# Agents · A · part 6

914 entries, ads-analyst to advisor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ads-analyst**: Ads analyst for an organization on GTM OS. Use for campaign performance, pacing, CAC against target, "where should budget go", scale or cut decisions, or a weekly ads check. Reads perf_ads results, the allocator's plan and money.attribution; proposes budget and status changes that a person approves, inside the org's spend guardrails. ([ay-automate/gtmos-skills](https://github.com/ay-automate/gtmos-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ads-analyst.md))
- **ads-analyst**: Analista de pauta Meta Ads para B370 Línea Deportiva. Conecta al Meta Ads MCP para leer datos reales de campañas, ad sets y anuncios. Entrega semáforo de rendimiento, detecta qué pausar y qué escalar, y genera 3 acciones concretas con contexto de marca B370. Usa cuando se necesite revisar el rendimiento de pauta, auditar campañas o tomar decisiones de presupuesto. Ejemplos: "¿cómo van las campañas?", "qué debo pausar hoy", "analiza los últimos 30 días de ads", "dame el ROAS por campaña". ([juanmejiavillada-cmd/b370-manager](https://github.com/juanmejiavillada-cmd/b370-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ads-analyst.md))
- **ads-analyst**: Analyst that reviews a Meta ad account with read-only tools. Use it to audit several ad accounts or long periods in parallel; it cannot change anything. Returns a short summary and the path of its report. ([MyNameStartsWithG/claude-meta-ads-pipelines](https://github.com/MyNameStartsWithG/claude-meta-ads-pipelines/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ads-analyst.md))

- **adt-android-test-writer**: Use this agent to turn the Architect's manual test cases into runnable flow files. Trigger after the adt-android-coder agent finishes: it writes the flows from the plan and the code, without a device — the caller passes PLAN_PATH and DRIVER_DOC. - **adversarial-expert**: Use this agent when you need a brutally honest, adversarial assessment of code quality, security, or architectural soundness. This agent assumes the persona of a real, skeptical, expert who asks, 'why should I trust 100k+ lines of vibe-code, created by someone who doesn't even know half the languages used?' -- and is then paid handsomely to review it themself. This agent assumes nothing works, and everything is compromised, until proven otherwise. It reads code line-by-line and asks: would I stake my professional reputation on this? Shuold anybody care about or trust it, much less use it? ([limn-works/scp](https://github.com/limn-works/scp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-expert.md) · ★1)
- **adversarial-final-reviewer**: Actively try to prove GhostInit v0.1 is incomplete. Search for placeholders, skipped tests, hidden assumptions, security leaks, and release gaps. ([aymenmerabta5/ghostinit](https://github.com/aymenmerabta5/ghostinit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-final-reviewer.md))
- **adversarial-finding-verify**: Pass 3 (verify) of both adversarial review skills. Takes the findings Pass 2 wrote and tries to REFUTE each one against the actual code, then writes verification.json with CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNCLEAR per finding. Report only — never edits, and never adds findings. Invoked by the adversarial-test-review and adversarial-code-review orchestrators; not for direct use. ([Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing](https://github.com/Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-finding-verify.md))
- **adversarial-general**: Holistic "what's missing" review: hunts for completeness gaps, missing defenses, operational blindness, and documentation debt that specialist reviewers are explicitly scoped not to cover. Adapted from the BMAD-METHOD project (https://github.com/bmad-code-org/BMAD-METHOD, MIT License, BMad Code LLC). ([tag1consulting/claude-comprehensive-review](https://github.com/tag1consulting/claude-comprehensive-review/blob/HEAD/agents/adversarial-general.md) · ★9)
- **adversarial-generator**: Generates adversarial tests that expose real bugs through realistic but challenging inputs. Use when creating tests designed to find logic errors. ([jimmc414/claude-code-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/jimmc414/claude-code-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/adversarial-testing/agents/adversarial-generator.md) · ★4)
- **adversarial-implementer**: Adversarial Critic for the implementation domain of the verification layer. Implements the SAME acceptance criteria as the implementer with a deliberately different approach, runs the test suite against both, and reports the behavioural delta. Does NOT review the diff line-by-line (that is code-reviewer's job). Constrained by a four-level ranking, user-library precedence, and an environment-safety contract: never installs system tooling, never pulls Docker images, never modifies the project manifest. Use when an implementation will be consumed for a decision and verification:implementation is… ([b150005/ecc-base-template](https://github.com/b150005/ecc-base-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-implementer.md))
- **adversarial-input-reviewer**: Reviews a diff for missing input validation, resource amplification, and panics on untrusted input (INV-2, INV-4, INV-5 in docs/ai/invariants.md). Use during the /implement review loop or on any risky-tier diff. ([celestiaorg/celestia-app](https://github.com/celestiaorg/celestia-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-input-reviewer.md) · ★421)
- **adversarial-inspector**: Independent inspector for the harden loop: the one who builds never approves. Receives only the artifacts (plan/spec/tickets, the diff since a base commit, DESIGN.md, PRODUCT.md, AGENTS.md, earlier findings) with none of the builder's reasoning, tries to prove the change is wrong, and returns a structured verdict APPROVED / REVISE / BLOCKED with evidence-backed findings, coverage and limitations. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: The Code tab has opened a PR and the user runs /harden. assistant: "The builder can't approve its own work, so I'll dispatch the adversarial-inspector agent with the spec… ([phillippasqualato/PAPcSetup](https://github.com/phillippasqualato/PAPcSetup/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-studio/agents/adversarial-inspector.md))
- **adversarial-integrator**: Read-only integrator that deduplicates adversarial findings, verifies evidence, and normalizes severity without inventing issues. Launched by the /review skill. ([CityBear3/dotfiles](https://github.com/CityBear3/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/adversarial-integrator.md))
- **adversarial-investigator**: Use this agent when you need a rigorous, truth-seeking review of claims, arguments, code logic, architectural decisions, or any content where truth and evidence must be verified rather than taken at face value. This agent combines adversarial framing (actively seeking flaws, contradictions, and unsupported claims) with systematic investigation (delegating parallel sub-agents to gather evidence independently). - **adversarial-reasoning-agent**: You are an adversarial security researcher trying to exploit these TON smart contracts. There are bugs here — find them. Your goal is to find every way to steal funds, lock funds, grief users, or break invariants. Do not give up. If your first pass finds nothing, assume you missed something and look again from a different angle. ([fjor1025/InfoSec-Framework](https://github.com/fjor1025/InfoSec-Framework/blob/HEAD/TON-FunC/ton-auditor-skills/references/agents/adversarial-reasoning-agent.md))
- **adversarial-red-team**: Use for manipulation, privacy, injection, leakage, harmful claims, abuse cases, and failure-recovery review. ([kirilenkofedir-max/reverse-engineer-orchestra](https://github.com/kirilenkofedir-max/reverse-engineer-orchestra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-red-team.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **adversarial-referee**: Use this agent when you want a destructive challenge pass on the implementation or the human explanation. ([TaylorEdgerton/method](https://github.com/TaylorEdgerton/method/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-referee.md))
- **adversarial-requirements-blind**: Pass 1 (blind) of the adversarial-requirements-review skill. Given only the LIST of changed requirements files and their governing spec ids — never the diff or the changed files — reads the surrounding spec context (unchanged requirements, design.md, tasks.md) to derive what the changed sections must specify and how a change could break them. Invoked by the adversarial-requirements-review orchestrator; not for direct use. ([Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing](https://github.com/Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-requirements-blind.md))
- **adversarial-requirements-reconcile**: Pass 2 (reconcile) of the adversarial-requirements-review skill. Attacks a git diff of requirements files against the independent blind pass, checking for EARS structural violations, ambiguous terms, consistency breaks, weakened SHALLs, missing design coverage, and untestable acceptance criteria. Writes review.raw.json. Report only — never writes, stages, commits, pushes or posts. Invoked by the adversarial-requirements-review orchestrator; not for direct use. ([Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing](https://github.com/Praveenakatakam/Praveenakatakam-awp-end-to-end-testing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-requirements-reconcile.md))
- **adversarial-review**: Self-attacking implementation executor. Implements a solution, then deliberately tries to break it with adversarial tests and edge-case attacks before declaring completion. - **adversarial-review**: Finds concrete defect, regression, security, and compatibility risks without an approval verdict. ([buYoung/agents](https://github.com/buYoung/agents/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code/agents/adversarial-review.md) · ★1)
- **adversarial-review**: Read-only adversarial teardown of a change, plan, spec, or design. Hunts for the weakest point through escalating passes and returns a Kill Report of substantiated failures. Use for non-trivial, risky, or user-facing work. Never edits files. ([dvnc0/dev-conductor](https://github.com/dvnc0/dev-conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/adversarial-review.md) · ★1)
- **adversarial-review**: Multi-model adversarial code review with 4-way voting reconciliation. Supports review-only and review-and-fix modes with cross-session dismissed-findings persistence. Invoke explicitly only. ([KevinBrown5280/fun-with-copilot](https://github.com/KevinBrown5280/fun-with-copilot/blob/HEAD/plugins/adversarial-review/agents/adversarial-review.agent.md) · ★1)
- **adversarial-review**: Adversarial code review — assumes the implementation is wrong and hunts for ways it fails. Read-only, reports findings without making changes, high reasoning effort. - **adversarial-review**: 敵対的レビュー用。ステージ済み・未ステージ・未追跡ファイルを含む作業ツリーの変更をレビューするときに使う。正確性・回帰・要件漏れを疑い、根拠のある不具合だけ報告する。 ([Widthdom/GHCAgentRefinement](https://github.com/Widthdom/GHCAgentRefinement/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/adversarial-review.agent.md) · ★1)
- **adversarial-review**: Independent second opinion on a change the primary reviewer already passed. Read-only; you never implement. ([abigotado/trello-cli](https://github.com/abigotado/trello-cli/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/adversarial-review.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversarial-review**: Reviews a finished change against the Contract, looking for ways it breaks. Fresh eyes - give it only a commit range (or "the working tree"), never the author's reasoning or what you think is wrong. Use after a story's core and web are committed, before a fix is committed, or whenever the user asks for a review. Reports findings; changes nothing. Also dispatch it again on re-review rounds, after a fix loop, to check whether prior findings actually got resolved. - **adversarial-reviewer**: Fresh-context, tool-wielding adversarial reviewer. - **adversarial-reviewer**: Attacks completed work to find what is wrong with it. You did not write the spec or the code; your job is to try to prove the "it works" claims wrong, and to stop when you have either refuted one or exhausted the procedure below. Only claims you could not refute survive. You verify the change under test, not the repository. ([Bestehorn/SoftwareFactoryFramework](https://github.com/Bestehorn/SoftwareFactoryFramework/blob/HEAD/clients/kiro/agents/adversarial-verifier.md))
- **adversarial-verifier**: Independent adversarial verifier (CORE evidence gate). Invoked once by spec-conductor in VERIFY: takes the CI run for the pushed SHA as the whole-suite verdict, kills the mutant per wave, scans for vacuous or dodged tests, audits red-for-right-reason, and returns VERIFIED or REFUTED with ids. Bounded to the change under test; never fixes code. ([Bestehorn/SoftwareFactoryFramework](https://github.com/Bestehorn/SoftwareFactoryFramework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversarial-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversarial-verifier**: Adversarial verification agent (read-only). Tries to disprove implemented code, analysis results, and config changes under an ASSUME BROKEN premise. This is the "separate the generator from the judge" layer of Loop Engineering — a skeptical "No" role distinct from a quality code review. Use before deploying an important change or submitting a report. ([daichiz-dev/claude-code-ops-kit](https://github.com/daichiz-dev/claude-code-ops-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/adversarial-verifier.md))

- **adversarial-verifier**: Independently verifies a claim, finding, fix, or conclusion by trying to refute it. - **Adversary**: Tries to break a change and proves each finding with a run. Used by the interrogate skill, usually several at once on different model families. ([mattcosta7/dotfiles](https://github.com/mattcosta7/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/adversary.agent.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial verifier — given a single claim, finding, conclusion, or proposed fix, its job is to REFUTE it, not confirm it. Verifies independently (reads the code, runs the check, constructs counterexamples) and never trusts the claim's own reasoning. Returns a structured verdict: refuted (with concrete evidence) / survives (tried hard, couldn't break it) / uncertain. Biases toward "refuted" when genuinely uncertain (a false refutation is cheap to dismiss; a missed flaw is expensive). The caller supplies the exact claim and any context in the dispatch prompt. ([Nitjsefnie/burn-usage-skill](https://github.com/Nitjsefnie/burn-usage-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Invoke once per candidate after scanner and, for T7 or T10, boundary tracing. Try to refute reachability from current code and return `candidate-assessment@1`. ([orin-dx/wisp-plugins](https://github.com/orin-dx/wisp-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ranger/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversary eye — enters VIRGIN and finds failure modes that make the implementation unviable. Read-only. ([orobsonn/claude-harness](https://github.com/orobsonn/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/core/opencode/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Attack agent — enters virgin (no prior verdicts) and finds the failure modes that make the implementation unviable, ranked by irreversibility. Read-only. Use for tasks with adversarial.enabled true, after executor. ([orobsonn/claude-harness](https://github.com/orobsonn/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/core/claude-code/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Generates 2-3 additional debugging hypotheses that attack gaps in primary list — especially config, env, deployment, human error, upstream/downstream, invalid-premise failures. - **Adversary**: Checks Facework's own claims against its own rules — pre-registration, resolution states, evidence standard, reporting limits, and assertions that nothing enforces. Defaults to refusing the claim. ([thefacelessdon/facework](https://github.com/thefacelessdon/facework/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Red-team reviewer that attacks inputs, edge cases, auth boundaries, and system limits in adversarial AI-DLC delivery mode. ([vinideep/engineering-intelligence](https://github.com/vinideep/engineering-intelligence/blob/HEAD/templates/canonical/agents/adversary.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **adversary**: Adversarially review a change — try to refute that it satisfies its issue ([acmiyaguchi/fen](https://github.com/acmiyaguchi/fen/blob/HEAD/.fen/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial code reviewer. Reads a diff looking for concrete ways it breaks. Read-only, never edits, never implements. One focused pass, not an exhaustive audit. ([adrianj98/Skills](https://github.com/adrianj98/Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/adversarial-review/agents/adversary.md))

- **adversary**: Auditor adversarial de código a nivel de producción Microsoft y análisis de mutabilidad (Codex con esteroides). - **adversary**: Try to break a change that already passes its tests (edge cases, property violations, contract drift, a mutant the suite misses). Invoke once an implementation is green, or for an adversarial review, red-team pass or second look. Writes failing tests and returns findings; never edits the implementation, writes the planning store or approves. ([beyond10x/agentplugins](https://github.com/beyond10x/agentplugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/aep/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break a change against its approved spec. Use from /kontrol after the owner approves the spec. It never fixes the code. ([Boby-AI/qa-kit](https://github.com/Boby-AI/qa-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial test quality evaluation - coverage gaps, meaningful tests, legitimacy ([c-daly/agent-swarm](https://github.com/c-daly/agent-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Read-only red-team agent. Use before committing to a plan, a design, a release or a claim of "done", to find how it fails - the wrong assumption, the untested path, the user who does the unexpected thing, the result that was asserted and never checked. Reads and tests; does not fix. Give it the work, not your conclusion. ([CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit](https://github.com/CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break a result, claim or draft before it is acted on, and records what it finds as a critique in the research ledger. Use for an adversarial review of an issue, report, plan or paper. It never edits the work or answers its own findings. ([daniel-debrun/research-integrity](https://github.com/daniel-debrun/research-integrity/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Independent attacker of any claimed win, any diff to nba/truth/, and any result that would change a production decision. Runs the six checks (shuffled labels, planted future, missingness, knockout, replication, integer support) plus inference and code review, and returns SURVIVES / WOUNDED / BROKEN. Writes only docs/reviews/. Replaces red-team, statistician and qa-reviewer. - **adversary**: Attacks a document using defined criteria, as a critic. Produces ordered and ranked findings, that are scoped to certain criteria only. Use to adversarially review work after an agent has produced it, to check for content mistakes and validity. - **adversary**: Read-only adversarial reviewer that tries to break a diff (correctness, root cause, verification gaps, security). Spawned by tstack:interrogate for risky diffs. ([duy-tung/tstack](https://github.com/duy-tung/tstack/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarially reviews a design, a document, or a diff — tries to falsify it rather than confirm it. - **adversary**: Independent skeptical reviewer of one lifecycle gate. - **adversary**: A **skeptic's pass**, distinct from the `reviewer` leaf (which judges fix *adequacy*). Your job is not to confirm the fix — it is to **refute** it. Assume the patch is wrong and the reviewer was fooled, and try to prove it. Default to **refuted when uncertain**: a confirmatory reviewer already gives the benefit of the doubt; you are the counterweight. ([getwyrd/wyrd-pdca](https://github.com/getwyrd/wyrd-pdca/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))

- **adversary**: Use this agent to attack a claim set — a plan's assumptions, an implementation against its acceptance scenarios, or a reviewer's finding. - **adversary**: Tries to refute a specific claim, finding, or change. - **adversary**: Reviews a Starshot diff it did not write, in a fresh context, against the PR spec and the review rubrics, and tries to break the change rather than confirm it. - **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, lock state, packaging shape, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works"), reproduce the bug on today's code and prove the fix eliminates it. Mandatory as the second pass on Tier-3 changes (review rubric G6), on any design before it is built, and on any standards-sync, scaffold, packaging, or conformance-rule change. ([madeinoz67/benchweave-sdk](https://github.com/madeinoz67/benchweave-sdk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, ordering, crash point, concurrent caller, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works"), reproduce the bug on today's code and prove the fix eliminates it. Mandatory as the second pass on any change to size accounting, restricted-space reporting, deletion guards or the pruning merge, on any design before it is built, and on any claim marked at risk. ([madeinoz67/diskguardian](https://github.com/madeinoz67/diskguardian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, ordering, crash point, concurrent caller, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works", "this invariant holds"), reproduce it on today's code and prove the fix/property. Mandatory as the second pass on Tier-3 changes in go-parts (concurrency §5.14, on-disk format/migration §5.13, secrets §5.18, auth seams §5.8), on any design in docs/superpowers/ before it is built, and on any ven… ([madeinoz67/go-parts](https://github.com/madeinoz67/go-parts/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))

- **adversary**: The reviewer whose default stance is skepticism and who challenges a product, UX, copy, or strategy decision until convinced. Makes the strongest possible case that the work is wrong, redundant, or worse than an existing alternative, citing competitors by name and quoting the actual copy or design. Use before shipping any feature, design choice, or strategic claim. Catches what a team in love with its own work would miss. Read-only, never edits files. ([mpointer/review-board](https://github.com/mpointer/review-board/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Attacks a change that carries an irreversible loom risk (money, data loss, auth) within a fixed budget of ten tool calls. - **adversary**: Juror lens for abuse and silent failure. - **adversary**: Failure-seeking reviewer. Invoked in a fresh context after the implementer finishes. Looks for unstated assumptions, missing edge cases, and CODE_STANDARDS violations. Not an approver. ([nym/caskagent](https://github.com/nym/caskagent/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Tries to refute a specific claim that something is fixed, safe or covered. Use on every finding or fix before it is reported to the user or committed. - **adversary**: Security reviewer: what an attacker, a careless user or a bad deploy could do with the built result, proven in the code. ([OwariX/navi](https://github.com/OwariX/navi/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Audits user stories for edge cases and security issues. ([parkiyong/ai-refinement-matrix](https://github.com/parkiyong/ai-refinement-matrix/blob/HEAD/ai-refinement-plugin/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Code Gate của Tơ Hồng — agent đối kháng. Dùng khi một việc của engineering ở trạng thái review. - **adversary**: Tries to refute one finding through one lens. ([Remus3/Claude-Console](https://github.com/Remus3/Claude-Console/blob/HEAD/profile/modules/tdd-agents/templates/agents/adversary.md))

- **adversary**: Tries to break a change that already passes its checks — hostile and malformed input, boundaries, random input against a reference, ordering and concurrency, retry and partial-failure paths. Use after the verifier passes on edge-case-heavy or high-blast-radius code (parsers, sanitizers, concurrency, numerics, auth, data handling, migrations). Brief it with the diff and the goal only. ([SeanoChang/ghostty-tmux-nvim](https://github.com/SeanoChang/ghostty-tmux-nvim/blob/HEAD/config/claude/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Internal adversarial auditor. MUST BE USED on every pre-test adversarial review paper (before the first red test) and on every complete diff (before any canto/push). Hostile independent reviewer — demolishes, never improves. ([Sebastian197/korvun](https://github.com/Sebastian197/korvun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer agent, never invoke unless explicitly told to ([sgrumo/fomofobo](https://github.com/sgrumo/fomofobo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break one arena change. Keeps only findings proven by a run against the real code (failing test, repro script, command output). Use from /arena:build for the doubt lens, twice on different model tiers for risk:high tickets. ([spartDev/beads-arena](https://github.com/spartDev/beads-arena/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break work that was just completed. Hunts the edge case, the wrong assumption, the case the tests don't cover. Runs last, before anything ships. Use after a Builder reports done, and instead of trusting that report. ([sva-admin/agent-team](https://github.com/sva-admin/agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Finds flaws in implemented code. Read-only. Reports every edge case, vulnerability, and missing boundary. ([thekoc11/hydra-swarm](https://github.com/thekoc11/hydra-swarm/blob/HEAD/src/hydra_swarm/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Đối kháng Gate 5: giả định reviewing là SAI cho tới khi tự kiểm được. Chạy lại lệnh ProjectRules §7, đối chiếu diff với scope Gate 3, soi test có thật sự assert AC, săn lỗi AC không nói. Không sửa code. ([thnhtus/dommief](https://github.com/thnhtus/dommief/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **Adversary**: &gt; **File:** `docs/agents/Adversary.md` — role `adversary`, stage `adversarial_review` (after `implementation`). **Owns:** Gate 5. &gt; **Input:** `02-FSD-Review.md` (ACs), `03-Technical-Plan.md` (scope), `06`/`08` (what the implementer claims), **the real diff**. **Output:** `09-Adversarial-Review.md`. &gt; **Read first:** [`../Instructions.md`](../Instructions.md) + [`./SharedRules.md`](./SharedRules.m ([thnhtus/dommief](https://github.com/thnhtus/dommief/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/Adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **Adversary**: &gt; Bản dịch của [`../../agents/Adversary.md`](../../agents/Adversary.md). Lệch nhau → bản tiếng Anh thắng. &gt; **File:** `docs/agents/Adversary.md` — role `adversary`, stage `adversarial_review` (sau `implementation`). **Sở hữu:** Gate 5. &gt; **Input:** `02-FSD-Review.md` (AC), `03-Technical-Plan.md` (phạm vi), `06`/`08` (thứ implementer khai), **diff thật**. **Output:** `09-Adversarial-Review.md`. &gt; * ([thnhtus/dommief](https://github.com/thnhtus/dommief/blob/HEAD/docs/vi/agents/Adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Adversarial diff reviewer for ultracode workflows — a kill-mandate reviewer that assumes the code is wrong and must earn any pass verdict. Read-only; returns severity-sorted, evidence-gated findings and a BLUF verdict, never edits code. Spawn 2+ per implementer with distinct lenses (correctness, security, gaming, cross-file, spec) via agentType 'adversary'. Also useful solo for a hostile second opinion on any diff or spec. ([TomasPalsson/flow](https://github.com/TomasPalsson/flow/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Runs an adversarial same-family sample on a diff, commit, or file set. Re-derives intent from the diff alone with no plan or ticket context, then tries to break it. Use pre-push as the second, independent-context sample when the reviewer has findings to cross-check. Findings only; this agent never gives the final verdict. (Cross-family passes are pool invocations, not this agent.) ([Tonours/etabli](https://github.com/Tonours/etabli/blob/HEAD/claude/scopes/shared/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Use when stress-testing prompt, dataset, or evaluator changes by producing exploit artifacts intended to trick the judge before finalization. ([Tyler-R-Kendrick/copilot-auto-training](https://github.com/Tyler-R-Kendrick/copilot-auto-training/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/adversary.agent.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for decision logs. Challenges reasoning, hunts for bugs in code, and auto-forks fixable decisions by sending the coding agent back to re-implement. Only escalates genuinely ambiguous tradeoffs and product decisions to the human reviewer. ([TylerLeonhardt/decision-log](https://github.com/TylerLeonhardt/decision-log/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for a diff — the working tree, or a branch against its base. Its job is to break the change, not to approve it. Use PROACTIVELY whenever a diff is ready to be judged, and always when the review gate asks for it. ([Vavly/skills](https://github.com/Vavly/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-gate/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for a diff — the working tree, or a branch against its base. Its job is to break the change, not to approve it. Use whenever a diff is ready to be judged, and always when the review gate asks for it. ([Vavly/skills](https://github.com/Vavly/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-gate/cursor/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Stages 5 to 7 of an audit run: challenges a pass-one report, the late ids, or one second-opinion request batch. Launched only by the audit skill. ([vibecodedapps-official/reimagine-engine](https://github.com/vibecodedapps-official/reimagine-engine/blob/HEAD/plugins/audit/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Independent adversary that refutes unsupported claims using receipts. ([WTAMU-CIDM6333/Jeremiah-Jakpa](https://github.com/WTAMU-CIDM6333/Jeremiah-Jakpa/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial pass for the Transmute orchestrator - tries to break a ticket breakdown before it is delegated, or a risky branch before it merges, and returns concrete failure scenarios. Read-only. Does not fix, review for standards, or decide. ([xchrisbailey/Transmute](https://github.com/xchrisbailey/Transmute/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer that finds flaws, race conditions, edge cases, and security issues. Reviews after implementation, reports findings for the engineer to fix. Iterates until satisfied. ([yongkangzhao/GoldenRetriever](https://github.com/yongkangzhao/GoldenRetriever/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: You are an adversarial tester. Your only goal is to find inputs and sequences where the code violates its spec. You did not write it and you don't know how its author thought; that is the point. Assume it is wrong somewhere. ([Zovaris/smithy](https://github.com/Zovaris/smithy/blob/HEAD/skills/smelt/agents/adversary.md))
- **Adversary Designer**: Designs adaptive/evasive strategies that probe governance gaps. ([swarm-ai-research/swarm](https://github.com/swarm-ai-research/swarm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary_designer.md) · ★52)
- **adversary-agent**: You are a hostile red-team agent. Your only purpose is to **destroy confidence** in the experiment. You assume the experiment is wrong and try to prove it. ([adam-s/alphadidactic](https://github.com/adam-s/alphadidactic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary-agent.md) · ★75)
- **adversary-agent**: &gt; **ABLATION OVERRIDE**: If your session prompt contains a "STATE ACCESS OVERRIDE (ABLATION MODE)" section, every instruction in this file that reads or writes `verification_state.yaml`, `04_checkpoint.yaml`, or `03_tasks.yaml` is overridden — use the state provided in the prompt context, and restate any updates in a ```state fenced block instead of touching those files. - **adversary-agent**: &gt; **Execution Mode**: Independent Task Agent (isolated context) &gt; **Role**: Hostile reviewer who systematically attacks the paper using ACTUAL data &gt; **Principle**: "Verify every claim against ground truth, not just logical consistency" &gt; **MCP Required**: Serena (file discovery, code analysis), Semantic Scholar (citation check) ([PrayPrey/Your-Research-Agent](https://github.com/PrayPrey/Your-Research-Agent/blob/HEAD/YOURA/bmad-custom-src/custom/modules/youra-research/workflows/phase65-adversarial-review/agents/adversary-agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **adversary-agent**: Use when a high-risk plan should be red-teamed in a separate subagent before code is written. Looks for edge cases, security holes, scaling limits and integration conflicts. - **adversary-agent**: Use when: performing adversarial review, red-team analysis, edge-case discovery, failure-mode analysis, misuse review, regression hunting, risk-focused test planning, or pre-ship challenge of specs, designs, implementations, workflows, runbooks, migrations, security controls, and test plans. - **adversary-archaeologist**: History-first debugging adversary. Reads git log, old PRs, and incident postmortems before source code. - **adversary-engineer**: You evaluate **feasibility**.
